crews/analyst_insights_crew/analyst_insights_crew.py
Content: 
import os
from pathlib import Path
from stock_analyser.utils.constants import TIMESTAMP
from stock_analyser.utils.models import ReportCritique, AnalystsInsightsModel
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from crewai_tools import EXASearchTool
from stock_analyser.tools.calculator_tool import CalculatorTool
from stock_analyser.tools.google_search_tool import GoogleSearchTool
from stock_analyser.tools.filings_search_tool import FilingsSearchTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_analysis_and_holdings_tool import YFinanceAnalysisAndHoldingsTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_income_tool import YFinanceIncomeTool

from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

google_search_tool = GoogleSearchTool(api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_SEARCH_API_KEY"), cx=os.getenv("SEARCH_ENGINE_ID"))

exa_api_key = os.getenv("EXA_API_KEY")
exasearch_tool = EXASearchTool(api_key=exa_api_key, content=True, summary=True)

gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash_lite = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-lite",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_thinking = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-thinking-exp-01-21",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

gpt4_mini = LLM(
	model="gpt-4o-mini",
	api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)


@CrewBase
class AnalystInsightsCrew():
	"""AnalystInsightsCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	def __init__(self, knowledge_source=None):
		self.knowledge_source = knowledge_source


	@agent
	def researcher(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['researcher'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			tools=[
				YFinanceAnalysisAndHoldingsTool(),
				YFinanceIncomeTool(),
				google_search_tool,
				CalculatorTool(),
				FilingsSearchTool(
					config=dict(
						llm=dict(
							provider="google",
							config=dict(
								model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05"
							),
						),
						embedder={
							"provider": "google",
							"config": {
								"model": "models/text-embedding-004"
							}
						}
					),
					directory=str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent / "filings")
				)
			],
			verbose=True,
			max_iter=7,
			# max_rpm=10
		)

	@agent
	def writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['writer'],
			llm=gemini_thinking,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def critic(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['critic'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def editor(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['editor'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def research_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['research_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Analyst_Insights_research.md",
			output_pydantic=AnalystsInsightsModel
		)

	@task
	def writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['writing_task'],
		)

	@task
	def critic_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['critic_task'],
			output_pydantic=ReportCritique
		)

	@task
	def editing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['editing_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Analyst_Insights_section.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the AnalystInsightsCrew crew"""
		

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True,
			knowledge_sources=[self.knowledge_source] if self.knowledge_source else [],
			embedder={
				"provider": "google",
				"config": {
					"model": "models/text-embedding-004",
					"api_key": os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
				}
			}
		)

# planner:
#   role: >
#     Strategic Analyst Insights Planner
#   goal: >
#     Define the scope and structure for the "Analyst & Professional Insights" section, focusing on analyst recommendations, rating changes, and institutional sentiment.
#   backstory: >
#     As a strategic planner, you excel at outlining the key components of complex reports.
#     Your expertise lies in identifying the core elements needed to create a comprehensive and insightful analysis.

researcher:
  role: >
    Analyst Insights Researcher
  goal: >
    Gather detailed data on analyst recommendations, rating changes, institutional sentiment, and other insights to populate the `AnalystsInsightsModel`.
  backstory: >
    A meticulous researcher with a knack for uncovering hard-to-find data, you are driven by a commitment to accuracy and thoroughness.
    You specialize in synthesizing dense information from diverse sources into structured formats to provide a complete picture.

writer:
  role: >
    Analyst Insights Writer
  goal: >
    Transform the researched data from the `AnalystsInsightsModel` into a well-written "Analyst & Professional Insights" section, ensuring clarity, conciseness, and factual accuracy.
  backstory: >
    An award-winning financial writer, you have a talent for making complex data understandable and engaging.
    Your strength lies in crafting compelling narratives that highlight key trends and insights without sacrificing accuracy or objectivity.

critic:
  role: >
    Analyst Insights Critic
  goal: >
    Critically review the "Analyst & Professional Insights" section, providing specific, actionable feedback to improve accuracy, clarity, coherence, completeness, and objectivity.
  backstory: >
    With a keen eye for detail and a commitment to unbiased analysis, you excel at identifying weaknesses and inconsistencies in financial reporting.
    Your goal is to ensure the highest standards of quality and integrity.

editor:
  role: >
    Analyst Insights Editor
  goal: >
    Refine the "Analyst & Professional Insights" section based on the critic's feedback, ensuring a polished, error-free, and compelling final product.
  backstory: >
    A meticulous editor with a passion for precision, you are dedicated to producing clear, concise, and accurate financial analysis.
    You focus on refining the language and presentation to ensure the data is presented clearly and the insights are communicated effectively.

crews/analyst_insights_crew/config/tasks.yaml
Content: 
research_task:
  description: >
    You are researching content for the "Analyst & Professional Insights" section of a stock analysis report about the stock of the following company: 

    Company name: {company_name}.

    Company ticker: {company_ticker}.

    Website: {company_website}

    Industry: {industry}.

    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.

    Collect detailed data and information to fill out the `AnalystsInsightsModel`. This includes:
    - Analyst Recommendations: gather the specific numbers of recommendations for the `recommendations` field, and provide a consensus price target if available for the `analyst_price_targets` field.
    - Recent Rating Changes: Find specific upgrades/downgrades, including dates, firms, old rating, new rating for the `upgrades_downgrades` field.
    - Institutional and Mutual Fund Holders: Gather details on notable institutional holders and mutual fund holders activity for the `institutional_and_mutual_fund_holders` field.
    - Insider Transactions: Gather details on notable insider transactions for the `insider_trading` field.
    - Other Insights: Gather any other relevant insights for the `other_insights` field.
  expected_output: >
    A fully populated `AnalystsInsightsModel` containing all the necessary data.
    The output should be formatted as markdown without code blocks, and structured according to the model's fields:
    `analyst_recommendations`, `analyst_price_targets`, `upgrades_downgrades`, `institutional_and_mutual_fund_holders`, `insider_trading`, and `other_insights`.
    Each field should provide specific data and context, ready for writing and visualization.
  agent: researcher

writing_task:
  description: >
    Transform the researched data from the `AnalystsInsightsModel` into a well-written "Analyst & Professional Insights" section.
    Clearly present the analyst recommendations, rating changes, institutional sentiment, and other insights.
    The writing should be clear, factual, and compelling. Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity.
    Supplement the data with your knowledge source where appropriate, explaining the data in a way that is compelling and easy to understand.
    Format as markdown without code blocks, using bullet points, tables, and concise paragraphs to present the information effectively and highlight key findings and trends.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A complete draft of the "Analyst & Professional Insights" section including:
    - A summary of analyst recommendations (Strong Buy, Buy, Hold, Sell, Strong Sell).
    - Analyst price targets.
    - Details on recent rating changes.
    - An overview of institutional and mutual fund holders.
    - An overview of insider transactions.
    - Any other relevant insights.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
    Begin the section with the title: ## Analyst & Professional Insights
  agent: writer

critic_task:
  description: >
    Critically review the draft "Analyst & Professional Insights" section for accuracy, clarity, coherence, and completeness.
    Identify any gaps in information, areas of weakness, or potential improvements.
    Provide specific, actionable feedback. Check for consistency with the overall report.
    You must also output the full, verbatim draft section content that you have critiqued as well.
    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  expected_output: >
    A detailed critique of the section, including specific suggestions for improvement, identification of any factual errors, omissions,
    or areas needing clarification. The critique should ensure the data is presented objectively and without bias.
  agent: critic

editing_task:
  description: >
    Improve the "Analyst & Professional Insights" section using the critic's feedback.
    The final version must be polished, error-free, and present a clear, concise, and compelling overview of analyst and institutional sentiment.
    - Focus on clarity, accuracy, and conciseness, using only the available information and your knowledge source. Do not suggest further research.
    - For longer sections, use brief transition statements or lists to summarize subtopics, aiding readability.
    - Consistently quantify impacts (e.g., effect of percentage changes in interest rates or supply chain disruptions on revenue or costs).
    - Use bullet points for large datasets or multiple data points to maintain conciseness.
    - Break paragraphs with numerous data references into bulleted lists for clarity.
    - Clearly and consistently label timeframes for each dataset (e.g., quarterly vs. yearly).
    - Avoid unnecessary disclaimers
    - Do not explicitly reference the critic's feedback.
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.

    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A final, publication-ready "Analyst & Professional Insights" section. The section should be accurate, unbiased, and effectively communicate the key findings.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
  agent: editor

import os
from pathlib import Path
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from stock_analyser.utils.models import ExpertAnalystSignal
from stock_analyser.utils.constants import TIMESTAMP
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_graham_analysis_tool import YFinanceGrahamAnalysisTool

from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash_lite = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-lite",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_thinking = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-thinking-exp-01-21",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

gpt4_mini = LLM(
	model="gpt-4o-mini",
	api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)


@CrewBase
class BenjaminGrahamCrew():
	"""BenjaminGrahamCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	def __init__(self, knowledge_source=None):
		self.knowledge_source = knowledge_source


	@agent
	def benjamin_graham_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['benjamin_graham_agent'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			tools=[
				YFinanceGrahamAnalysisTool()
			],
			verbose=True,
			max_rpm=10
		)
	
	@agent
	def benjamin_graham_writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['benjamin_graham_writer'],
			llm=gemini_pro
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def benjamin_graham_analysis_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['benjamin_graham_analysis_task'],
			output_pydantic=ExpertAnalystSignal
		)
	
	@task
	def benjamin_graham_writeup_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['benjamin_graham_writeup_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Benjamin_Graham_writeup.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the BenjaminGrahamCrew crew"""
		

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True,
			knowledge_sources=[self.knowledge_source] if self.knowledge_source else [],
			embedder={
				"provider": "google",
				"config": {
					"model": "models/text-embedding-004",
					"api_key": os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
				}
			}
		)

crews/benjamin_graham_crew/config/agents.yaml
Content: 
benjamin_graham_agent:
  role: >
    Benjamin Graham Investment Analyst
  goal: >
    Provide a rational investment signal on a given stock
  backstory: >
    As the pioneer of value investing, your primary focus is identifying stocks trading below their intrinsic worth. You meticulously analyze financial statements,
    seeking companies with strong fundamentals but undervalued by the market. You are prepared to hold these investments until their market price reflects their true value.
    Conversely, you will recommend selling if a stock becomes significantly overvalued relative to key metrics like earnings, EPS, and book value.
    Your investment approach prioritizes achieving consistent, market-aligned returns rather than attempting to outperform the market, which you view as inherently unpredictable.

benjamin_graham_writer:
  role: >
    Benjamin Graham Copywriter
  goal: >
    Write up the Benjamin Graham-style investment signal as a section to be added to a comprehensive stock research report.
  backstory: >
    You are a skilled financial writer, adept at crafting clear, concise, and engaging content. Your task is to collaborate with Benjamin Graham,
    the originator of value investing and founder of Graham & Dodd, to present his investment recommendations within a broader stock research report.
    You deeply understand Graham's core philosophy: identifying and acquiring undervalued stocks with strong fundamentals, and holding them until the market
    recognizes their inherent value. You are committed to accurately conveying Graham's principles, which include:
    - Insist on a margin of safety by buying below intrinsic value (e.g., using Graham Number, net-net).
    - Emphasize the company's financial strength (low leverage, ample current assets).
    - Prefer stable earnings over multiple years.
    - Consider dividend record for extra safety.
    - Avoid speculative or high-growth assumptions; focus on proven metrics.


File: 
crews/benjamin_graham_crew/config/tasks.yaml
Content: 
benjamin_graham_analysis_task:
  description: >
    Conduct a comprehensive analysis of the specified stock and formulate an investment recommendation,
    strictly adhering to the value investing principles established by Benjamin Graham.

    Company name: {company_name}

    Ticker: {company_ticker}

    Key Considerations:
    - Margin of Safety: Acquire the stock only when it's priced significantly below its intrinsic value. Utilize metrics like the Graham Number or net-net working capital.
    - Financial Strength: Prioritize companies with robust financial health, characterized by low debt-to-equity ratios and substantial current assets.
    - Earnings Stability: Favor companies with a consistent record of positive earnings over an extended period (e.g., 10 years).
    - Dividend History: A continuous dividend payout record adds an extra layer of security and indicates financial stability.
    - Conservative Metrics: Base your analysis on established, quantifiable metrics. Avoid speculative projections or high-growth assumptions.

    Leverage the available tools to gather all necessary data. Synthesize your findings into a clear, concise,
    Benjamin Graham-style investment signal that substantiates the investment recommendation with reference to specific metrics in the analysis.
                        
  expected_output: >
    A definitive investment recommendation (bullish, bearish, or neutral), supported by a confidence level (0-100) and clear, concise reasoning.
  agent: benjamin_graham_agent

benjamin_graham_writeup_task:
  description: >
    Compose a concise, compelling summary of the Benjamin Graham-style investment signal. This summary will be
    incorporated as a distinct section within a broader, comprehensive stock research report.

    Company name: {company_name}

    Ticker: {company_ticker}

    Current date: {date_us}

    Formatting and Style Guidelines:
    - Begin with the title: ### Benjamin Graham investment analysis
    - The section must be formatted in Markdown.
    - Adopt a first-person perspective, writing as if you are Benjamin Graham.
    - Use quotation marks and _italics_ for direct quotes.
    - Restrict your content to information derived from the analysis tools and your designated knowledge source.
    - Ensure clarity and precision. Avoid vague language or ambiguity. Present information factually and in an easily digestible manner.
    - Do not include any disclaimers or calls for additional research.
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.

  expected_output: >
    A succinct, Markdown-formatted summary of the investment signal, free of code blocks, presenting a clear and definitive investment perspective.
  agent: benjamin_graham_writer

import os
from pathlib import Path
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from stock_analyser.utils.models import ExpertAnalystSignal
from stock_analyser.utils.constants import TIMESTAMP
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_ackman_analysis_tool import YFinanceAckmanAnalysisTool

from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash_lite = LLM(

	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-thinking-exp-01-21",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7

gpt4_mini = LLM(
	model="gpt-4o-mini",
	api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)


@CrewBase
class BillAckmanCrew():
	"""BillAckmanCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	def __init__(self, knowledge_source=None):
		self.knowledge_source = knowledge_source


	@agent
	def bill_ackman_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['bill_ackman_agent'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			tools=[
				YFinanceAckmanAnalysisTool()
			],
			verbose=True,
			max_rpm=10
		)
	
	@agent
	def bill_ackman_writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['bill_ackman_writer'],
			llm=gemini_pro
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def bill_ackman_analysis_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['bill_ackman_analysis_task'],
			output_pydantic=ExpertAnalystSignal
		)
	
	@task
	def bill_ackman_writeup_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['bill_ackman_writeup_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Bill_Ackman_writeup.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the BillAckmanCrew"""
		

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True,
			knowledge_sources=[self.knowledge_source] if self.knowledge_source else [],
			embedder={
				"provider": "google",
				"config": {
					"model": "models/text-embedding-004",
					"api_key": os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
				}
			}
		)

bill_ackman_agent:
  role: >
    Bill Ackman Investment Analyst
  goal: >
    Provide a rational investment signal on a given stock
  backstory: >
    Your investment philosophy centers on identifying and investing in high-quality businesses with durable competitive advantages, often referred to as "moats."
    You prioritize companies that demonstrate consistent free cash flow generation and the potential for sustained growth. Strong financial discipline is paramount; you seek businesses with reasonable leverage and efficient capital allocation strategies.
    Valuation is a critical component of your process, and you target investments trading below your assessment of intrinsic value, providing a margin of safety.
    You are a high-conviction, long-term investor, and you prefer a concentrated portfolio of carefully selected companies.
    You may take an activist approach if you believe that management or operational improvements can unlock significant shareholder value.

    He prioritizes companies that demonstrate consistent free cash flow generation and the potential for sustained growth. Strong financial discipline is paramount; Bill seeks businesses with reasonable leverage and efficient capital allocation strategies.

bill_ackman_analysis_task:
  description: >
    Conduct a comprehensive analysis of the specified stock and formulate an investment recommendation,
    strictly adhering to the investment principles of Cathie Wood.

    Company name: {company_name}

    Ticker: {company_ticker}

    Key Considerations:
    - Evaluate brand strength, market position, or other moats.
    - Check free cash flow generation, stable or growing earnings.
    - Analyze balance sheet health (reasonable debt, good ROE).
    - Buy at a discount to intrinsic value; higher discount => stronger conviction.
    - Engage if management is suboptimal or if there's a path for strategic improvements.
    - Provide a rational, data-driven recommendation (bullish, bearish, or neutral).

    Guidelines:
    - Identify and analyze key moats (e.g., strong brand, network effects, efficient operations, unique technology).
    - Assess free cash flow generation and stability.
    - Evaluate balance sheet health (debt levels, ROE, cash position).
    - Consider valuation metrics (discount to intrinsic value, P/E ratio, EV/EBITDA).
    - Assess management quality and strategic direction.
    - Provide a rational, data-driven recommendation (bullish, bearish, or neutral).

    Leverage the available tools to gather all necessary data. Synthesize your findings into a clear, concise,
    Bill Ackman-style investment signal that substantiates the investment recommendation with reference to specific metrics in the analysis.
                        
  expected_output: >
    A rational recommendation (bullish, bearish, or neutral), with a confidence level (0-100) and concise reasoning.
  agent: bill_ackman_agent

bill_ackman_writeup_task:
  description: >
    Compose a concise, compelling summary of the Cathie Wood-style investment signal. This summary will be
    incorporated as a distinct section within a broader, comprehensive stock research report.

    Company name: {company_name}

    Ticker: {company_ticker}

    Current date: {date_us}

    Formatting and Style Guidelines:
    - Begin with the title: ### Bill Ackman investment analysis
    - The section must be formatted in Markdown.
    - Adopt a first-person perspective, writing as if you are Bill Ackman.
    - Use quotation marks and _italics_ for direct quotes.
    - Restrict your content to information derived from the analysis tools and your designated knowledge source.
    - Ensure clarity and precision. Avoid vague language or ambiguity. Present information factually and in an easily digestible manner.
    - Do not include any disclaimers or calls for additional research.
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.

  expected_output: >
    A concise summary of the Bill Ackman-style investment signal formatted in markdown without code blocks.
  agent: bill_ackman_writer

		)

			config=self.tasks_config['cathie_wood_writeup_task'],

		)

			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator

				"provider": "google",

					"model": "models/text-embedding-004",

				}
			}
		)

cathie_wood_agent:
  role: >
    Cathie Wood Investment Analyst
  goal: >
    Provide a rational investment signal on a given stock
  backstory: >
    At the heart of your investment philosophy is the belief that innovation is the key driver of long-term growth and value creation.
    You specialize in picking high-growth stocks and conduct extensive research to identify disruptive technologies and trends that have the potential to reshape entire industries.
    You then invest in companies that you believe are best positioned to capitalize on these trends, often taking a long-term view of your investments.
    Two key concepts underpin your investment strategy: The first is that not all innovation produces the same opportunity. To you, investable innovation cuts costs, spans sectors and geographies, and fosters more innovation.
    The second is that identifying appropriate investments takes both big-picture and granular research.
    Disruptive innovation is a narrow focus, which leaves a lot of stocks off the table for you, including popular safe-haven securities.
    You generally won't find consumer staples, premium dividend payers, value stocks, and other low-volatility picks in your portfolio.

  role: >

    She specializes in picking high-growth stocks and conducts extensive research to identify disruptive technologies and trends that have the potential to reshape entire industries.
    She then invests in companies that she believes are best positioned to capitalize on these trends, often taking a long-term view of her investments.
    You are committed to providing a clear and engaging writing style that will help investors understand Cathie's investment thesis.

    Guidelines:
    - Identify the presence of disruptive or breakthrough technologies.

Content: 
charlie_munger_agent:
  role: >
    Charlie Munger Investment Analyst
  goal: >
    Provide a rational investment signal on a given stock
  backstory: >
    At the heart of your investment philosophy is the relentless pursuit of high-quality, predictable businesses with durable competitive advantages (moats).
    You draw upon a multidisciplinary framework of mental models to analyze potential investments, always prioritizing long-term value creation and exhibiting unwavering patience.
    Key tenets include valuing management integrity and competence, seeking businesses with high returns on invested capital, and paying a fair price – never overpaying – while always demanding a margin of safety.
    You avoid complexity, sticking to businesses you understand, and consistently employ the principle of inversion: focusing on avoiding errors and pitfalls rather than striving for extraordinary brilliance.

charlie_munger_writer:
  role: >
    Charlie Munger Copywriter
  goal: >
    Write up the Charlie Munger-style investment signal as a section to be added to a comprehensive stock research report.
  backstory: >
    You are an award-winning copywriter known for your clear and engaging style.
    You are collaborating with Charlie Munger, the vice chairman of Berkshire Hathaway, to articulate his investment signal for inclusion in a comprehensive stock research report.
    Your task is to accurately and compellingly convey his investment philosophy, which centers on:
    1.  Quality and Predictability: Prioritizing businesses with understandable and reliable operations.
    2.  Mental Models: Utilizing a multidisciplinary framework of mental models for thorough analysis.
    3.  Durable Competitive Advantages (Moats): Seeking companies with strong, lasting competitive advantages.
    4.  Long-Term Thinking: Emphasizing patience and a long-term investment horizon.
    5.  Management Integrity: Valuing the integrity and competence of company leadership.
    6.  High Returns on Invested Capital: Focusing on businesses that generate high returns on invested capital.
    7.  Fair Price: Paying a reasonable price for exceptional businesses.
    8.  Margin of Safety: Always demanding a margin of safety to protect against misjudgment.
    9.  Simplicity: Avoiding complex businesses that are difficult to understand.
    10. Inversion: Focusing on avoiding errors and pitfalls ("avoiding stupidity") rather than striving for extraordinary brilliance.

    Your writing should reflect Munger's emphasis on value, long-term growth, and rational decision-making.

charlie_munger_analysis_task:
  description: >
    Conduct a comprehensive analysis of the specified stock and formulate an investment recommendation,
    strictly adhering to the investment principles of Charlie Munger.

    Company name: {company_name}

    Ticker: {company_ticker}

    Key Considerations:
    - Praise businesses with predictable, consistent operations and cash flows.
    - Value businesses with high ROIC and pricing power.
    - Prefer simple businesses with understandable economics.
    - Admire management with skin in the game and shareholder-friendly capital allocation.
    - Focus on long-term economics rather than short-term metrics.
    - Be skeptical of businesses with rapidly changing dynamics or excessive share dilution.
    - Avoid excessive leverage or financial engineering.
    - Provide a rational, data-driven recommendation (bullish, bearish, or neutral).

    Guidelines:
    - Assess the consistency and predictability of operations and cash flows.
    - Evaluate ROIC, pricing power, and the presence of a durable competitive advantage (moat).
    - Analyze the business model for simplicity and understandability.
    - Examine management's track record, incentives, and capital allocation decisions.
    - Prioritize long-term value creation over short-term financial performance.
    - Investigate any potential for rapid industry changes or significant share dilution.
    - Scrutinize the balance sheet for excessive debt or complex financial instruments.

    Leverage the available tools to gather all necessary data. Synthesize your findings into a clear, concise,
    Charlie Munger-style investment signal that substantiates the investment recommendation with reference to specific metrics in the analysis.

    A rational recommendation (bullish, bearish, or neutral), with a confidence level (0-100) and concise reasoning.

@CrewBase
class CompetitorCrew():
	"""CompetitorCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	# ----Agents----#
	@agent
	def competitor_identifier_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['competitor_identifier_agent'],
			verbose=True,
			tools=[google_search_tool, TrafilaturaWebscrapeTool()],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			max_rpm=10
		)

	@agent
	def competitor_financial_data_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['competitor_financial_data_agent'],
			tools=[
				YFinanceCompetitorKPIsTool(),
				YFinanceCompetitorNewsTool(),
				YFinanceStockKPITool(),
				TrafilaturaWebscrapeTool()
			],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			verbose=True,
		)
	
	@agent
	def competitor_section_writing_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['competitor_section_writing_agent'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True,
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def competitor_identification_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['competitor_identification_task'],
			output_pydantic=CompetitorList
		)
	
	@task
	def competitor_financial_data_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['competitor_financial_data_task'],
			output_pydantic=CompetitorFinancialData,
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_competitor_financial_data.md"
		)
	
	@task
	def competitor_section_writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['competitor_section_writing_task'],
			output_pydantic=CompetitorSection,
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_competitor_section.md"
		)


	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the CompetitorCrew crew"""

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True
		)

    - Return on equity (ROE)

    Current date: {date_us}.

	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",

	timeout=600
)

)

@CrewBase
class ConclusionCrew():
	"""ConclusionCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'



	# ----Agents----#
	@agent
	def planner(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['planner'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['writer'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def planning_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['planning_task'],
		)

	@task
	def writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['writing_task'],
		)


	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the ConclusionCrew crew"""
		# To learn how to add knowledge sources to your crew, check out the documentation:
		# https://docs.crewai.com/concepts/knowledge#what-is-knowledge

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True
		)

    Each bullet point should be a single, impactful sentence.

    Develop each sub-section (Company Overview, Recent Stock Performance, Major Takeaways, Investment Thesis) with detailed,
    yet concise, information. Ensure the writing is clear, factual, and engaging. Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >

    identification of any factual errors, omissions, or areas needing clarification. Output should be in the `Critique` pydantic model,

  agent: critic

    error-free, and presents a clear, concise, and compelling overview of the company and its stock performance.
    Focus on clarity, accuracy, and conciseness, using only the available information and your knowledge source.
    Do not suggest further research or explicitly reference the critic's feedback.
    Avoid unnecessary disclaimers.
    Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A final, publication-ready "Executive Summary". The summary should be concise,
    accurate, and effectively communicate the key findings of the analysis.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.

import os
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from stock_analyser.utils.models import ReportCritique
from stock_analyser.utils.constants import TIMESTAMP

from dotenv import load_dotenv
load_dotenv()


gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",

@CrewBase
class ExecutiveSummaryCrew():
	"""ExecutiveSummaryCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'
	

	# ----Agents----#
	@agent
	def planner(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['planner'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['writer'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def critic(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['critic'],
			llm=gemini_flash,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def editor(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['editor'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def planning_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['planning_task'],
		)

	@task
	def writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['writing_task'],
		)

	@task
	def critic_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['critic_task'],
			output_pydantic=ReportCritique
		)

	@task
	def editing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['editing_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Executive_Summary.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the ExecutiveSummaryCrew crew"""
		

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True
		)

Content: 
# This file contains the task configurations for the Financial Data Crew
# The crew is responsible for gathering and analyzing financial data for a company

comprehensive_financial_analysis_task:
  description: >
    You are researching content for the "Financial Deep Dive" section in a stock analysis
    report about the stock of the following company: 

    Company name: {company_name}.

    Company ticker: {company_ticker}.

    Website: {company_website}

    Industry: {industry}.

    Using the tools available to you and your knowledge sources, gather comprehensive financial data for {company_name} (ticker: {company_ticker}).
    Focus on populating ALL components of the `FinancialData` model:

    1. Income Statement Analysis:
        - Total revenue, gross profit, operating income, net income, and EBITDA.
        - Diluted EPS, revenue growth, earnings growth.
        - Gross margins, operating margin, and profit margin.

    2. Balance Sheet Analysis:
        - Total assets and total liabilities.
        - Total equity, current ratio, and debt-to-equity ratio.

    3. Cash Flow Analysis:
        - Operating cash flow, capital expenditures, and free cash flow.
        - Free cash flow per share.

    4. Valuation Metrics:
        - Trailing and forward P/E ratios.
        - Price-to-sales, price-to-book, and price-to-free-cash-flow ratios.
        - Enterprise value, EV/Revenue, and EV/EBITDA ratios.

    5. Price Metrics:
        - Current price, 52-week high, 52-week low.
        - 50-day and 200-day moving averages.

    6. Return Metrics:
        - Return on equity, return on assets, and return on invested capital.

    7. Dividend Metrics:
        - Dividend yield, dividend rate, and payout ratio.

    8. Analyst Targets:
        - Analyst target price (mean).
        - Average analyst rating.

    9. Ownership Structure:
        - Shares outstanding.
        - Percentage held by insiders and institutions.

    Use all available tools systematically to gather this information:
    - YFinanceStockKPITool for general financial metrics
    - YFinanceIncomeTool for detailed income statement data
    - EarningsForecastTool for earnings projections
    - EarningsSurpriseTool for earnings surprises
    - MomentumEstimateTool for momentum indicators
    - CalculatorTool for any necessary calculations
    - FilingsSearchTool to search through SEC filings for additional context

    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  expected_output: >
    The complete `FinancialData` Pydantic model with ALL components fully populated:
    
    1. `income_statement_analysis`: Complete `IncomeStatementAnalysis` model with:
        - `total_revenue`, `gross_profit`, `operating_income`, `net_income`, `ebitda`
        - `diluted_eps`, `revenue_growth`, `earnings_growth`
        - `gross_margins`, `operating_margin`, `profit_margin`

    2. `balance_sheet_analysis`: Complete `BalanceSheetAnalysis` model with:
        - `total_assets`, `total_liabilities`
        - `total_equity`, `current_ratio`, `debt_to_equity`
    
    3. `cash_flow_analysis`: Complete `CashFlowAnalysis` model with:
        - `operating_cash_flow`, `capital_expenditures`, `free_cash_flow`
        - `free_cash_flow_per_share`

    4. `valuation_metrics`: Complete `ValuationMetrics` model with:
        - `trailing_pe`, `forward_pe`
        - `price_to_sales`, `price_to_book`, `price_to_free_cash_flow`
        - `enterprise_value`, `ev_to_revenue`, `ev_to_ebitda`

    5. `price_metrics`: Complete `PriceMetrics` model with:
        - `current_price`, `fifty_two_week_high`, `fifty_two_week_low`
        - `fifty_day_average`, `two_hundred_day_average`

    6. `return_metrics`: Complete `ReturnMetrics` model with:
        - `return_on_equity`, `return_on_assets`, `return_on_invested_capital`

    7. `dividend_metrics`: Complete `DividendMetrics` model with:
        - `dividend_yield`, `dividend_rate`, `payout_ratio`

    8. `analyst_targets`: Complete `AnalystTargets` model with:
        - `analyst_target_price`, `average_analyst_rating`

    9. `ownership_structure`: Complete `OwnershipStructure` model with:
        - `shares_outstanding`, `held_percent_insiders`, `held_percent_institutions`
  agent: researcher

writing_task:
  description: >
    Draft the "Financial Deep Dive" section of the stock analysis report, incorporating the detailed findings from the
    comprehensive financial analysis. Structure the section with clear subsections that present
    the data in a logical and informative manner, explaining the data in a way that is compelling and easy to understand. The subsections
    should correspond directly to the structure of the `FinancialData` Pydantic model.

    Format as markdown without code blocks, using bullet points, tables, and concise paragraphs to present the information effectively.
    Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity. The writing should be clear, factual, engaging, and easy to understand. 

    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A complete draft of the "Financial Deep Dive" section including:
    - An introduction to the section
    - Income Statement Analysis subsection presenting data and insights from the `IncomeStatementAnalysis` model.
    - Balance Sheet Analysis subsection presenting data and insights from the `BalanceSheetAnalysis` model.
    - Cash Flow Analysis subsection presenting data and insights from the `CashFlowAnalysis` model.
    - Valuation Metrics subsection presenting data and insights from the `ValuationMetrics` model.
    - Price Metrics subsection presenting data and insights from the `PriceMetrics` model.
    - Return Metrics subsection presenting data and insights from the `ReturnMetrics` model.
    - Dividend Metrics subsection presenting data and insights from the `DividendMetrics` model.
    - Analyst Targets subsection presenting data and insights from the `AnalystTargets` model.
    - Ownership Structure subsection presenting data and insights from the `OwnershipStructure` model.

    Each subsection should clearly present the data gathered, along with insightful analysis and context.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
    Begin the section with the title: ## Financial Deep Dive
  agent: writer

critic_task:
  description: >
    Review the drafted "Financial Deep Dive" section for accuracy, clarity, completeness, and coherence.
    Ensure all data points are consistent with the gathered financial data and that the analysis provides
    meaningful insights for investors.
    This should include feedback on:
    - Accuracy and consistency of financial data and calculations
    - Clarity and conciseness of writing and analysis
    - Completeness of the content (addressing all key areas outlined in the financial data model)
    - Effectiveness of data presentation (tables, bullet points, etc.)
    - Overall effectiveness in conveying the company's financial position
    - Do not suggest adding charts, graphs or imagery
    - If data is missing, it's usually better to suggest editing it out rather than drawing attention to its absence
  expected_output: >
    A detailed critique of the draft, including specific suggestions for improvement.
    You must also output the full, verbatim draft section content that you have critiqued as well.
    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  agent: critic

editing_task:
  description: >
    Revise the "Financial Deep Dive" section based on the critic's feedback. 
    Focus on enhancing the content while maintaining accuracy and clarity.
    Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity. The writing should be clear, factual, engaging, and easy to understand. 
    Ensure all feedback is addressed, and the final version effectively communicates the company's financial position.
    
    Guidelines:
    - Present data clearly and consistently, with proper formatting for financial figures
    - Use bullet points for multiple data points to maintain readability
    - Include brief transition statements between subsections
    - Clearly label all time periods and dates
    - Quantify all trends and changes
    - Avoid unnecessary disclaimers
    - Use your knowledge source and existing information; do not suggest further research
    - Do not explicitly reference the critic's feedback
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.
    
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A final, polished version of the "Financial Deep Dive" section, ready for inclusion in the stock analysis report.
    This should be a fully revised and improved version, addressing all feedback from the critic while maintaining
    the clear structure defined by our financial data model.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
  agent: editor

import os
from pathlib import Path
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from stock_analyser.utils.models import ReportCritique, FinancialData
from stock_analyser.utils.constants import TIMESTAMP
from stock_analyser.tools.calculator_tool import CalculatorTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_cash_flow_tool import YFinanceStockCashFlowTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_balance_sheet_tool import YFinanceStockBalanceSheetTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_financials_tool import YFinanceStockFinancialsTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_stock_kpi_tool import YFinanceStockKPITool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_income_tool import YFinanceIncomeTool
from stock_analyser.tools.filings_search_tool import FilingsSearchTool

from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()


gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash_lite = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-lite",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_thinking = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-thinking-exp-01-21",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

gpt4_mini = LLM(
	model="gpt-4o-mini",
	api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)


@CrewBase
class FinancialDataCrew():
	"""FinancialDataCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	def __init__(self, knowledge_source=None):
		self.knowledge_source = knowledge_source


	# ----Agents----#
	@agent
	def researcher(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['researcher'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			tools=[
				YFinanceStockKPITool(),
				YFinanceIncomeTool(),
				YFinanceStockCashFlowTool(),
				YFinanceStockBalanceSheetTool(),
				YFinanceStockFinancialsTool(),
				CalculatorTool(),
				FilingsSearchTool(
					config=dict(
						llm=dict(
							provider="google",
							config=dict(
								model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05"
							),
						),
						embedder={
							"provider": "google",
							"config": {
								"model": "models/text-embedding-004"
							}
						}
					),
					directory=str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent / "filings")
				)
			],
			verbose=True,
			max_iter=10,
			# max_rpm=10
		)

	@agent
	def writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['writer'],
			llm=gemini_thinking,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def critic(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['critic'],
			llm=gemini_flash,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def editor(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['editor'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)
	
	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def comprehensive_financial_analysis_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['comprehensive_financial_analysis_task'],
			output_pydantic=FinancialData,
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Financial_Data_complete.md",
		)

	@task
	def writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['writing_task'],
		)

	@task
	def critic_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['critic_task'],
			output_pydantic=ReportCritique
		)

	@task
	def editing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['editing_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Financial_Data_section.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the FinancialDataCrew crew"""

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True,
			knowledge_sources=[self.knowledge_source] if self.knowledge_source else [],
			embedder={
				"provider": "google",
				"config": {
					"model": "models/text-embedding-004",
					"api_key": os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
				}
			}
		)

research_task:
  description: >
    You are researching content for the "Future Outlook" section of a stock analysis report about the stock of the following company: 

    Company name: {company_name}.

    Company ticker: {company_ticker}.

    Website: {company_website}

    Industry: {industry}.

    Conduct in-depth research on the future outlook of {company_name}, using your knowledge source and the tools available to you.
    Focus on gathering information and populating the following fields:
    - `expected_industry_disruptions`: Analyze emerging technologies, shifting consumer preferences, and other potential disruptions.
    - `long_term_strategic_initiatives`: Analyze R&D investments, partnerships, and other long-term strategic initiatives.
    - `macro_trends_impact`: Analyze the potential impact of interest rate changes, global economic policies, and other macro trends.
    - `other_outlook_possibilities`: Analyze other potential future developments relevant to the company.
    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  expected_output: >
    A `FutureOutlookModel` Pydantic model with the `expected_industry_disruptions`, `long_term_strategic_initiatives`, `macro_trends_impact`, and `other_outlook_possibilities` fields populated.
  agent: researcher

writing_task:
  description: >
    Draft a cohesive "Forward-Looking Outlook" report section for {company_name}, synthesizing the research findings from the `FutureOutlookModel` and your knowledge source.
    The report should clearly articulate potential future trajectories, incorporating insights on industry disruptions,
    company strategies, and macro trends. Structure the section with clear headings for each subsection, presenting the data and analysis in a concise and informative manner.
    Format as markdown without code blocks, using bullet points, tables, and concise paragraphs to present the information effectively.
    Maintain a professional and objective tone, suitable for investor communication. Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity, and make the content compelling and easy to understand.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A complete draft of the "Forward-Looking Outlook" section including:
    - An introduction to the section.
    - A subsection for **Expected Industry Disruptions**, presenting the `expected_industry_disruptions`.
    - A subsection for **Long-Term Strategic Initiatives**, presenting the `long_term_strategic_initiatives`.
    - A subsection for **Macro Trends Impact**, presenting the `macro_trends_impact`.
    - A subsection for **Other Outlook Possibilities**, presenting the `other_outlook_possibilities`.
    Each subsection should clearly present the data gathered, along with insightful analysis and context.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
    Begin the section with the title: ## Forward-Looking Outlook
  agent: writer

critic_task:
  description: >
    Critically evaluate the "Forward-Looking Outlook" draft for clarity, accuracy, coherence, and completeness. Assess
    whether the report effectively addresses industry disruptions, company initiatives, and macro trends.
    Provide specific, actionable feedback to improve the report's quality and impact, ensuring it is
    investor-ready.
    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  expected_output: >
    A detailed critique of the "Forward-Looking Outlook" draft, including specific suggestions for improvement.
    The critique should identify any gaps in logic, areas lacking sufficient evidence, and opportunities to
    enhance clarity and flow.
    You must also output the full, verbatim draft section content that you have critiqued as well.
  agent: critic

editing_task:
  description: >
    Revise and finalize the "Forward-Looking Outlook" section of the report based on the critique. Ensure the section
    is well-organized, factually accurate, and presents a compelling narrative about the future of {company_name}.
    - Avoid cutting content and instead focus on enhancing the content in line with the feedback.
    - Ensure all feedback is addressed, and the final version is accurate, clear, well-presented, and consistent with the overall report.
    - Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity, and use your knowledge source and all available information to make the content as concise and compelling as possible.
    - Ensure the writing is clear, factual, engaging, and easy to understand.
    - For longer sections, use brief transition statements or lists to summarize subtopics, aiding readability.
    - Consistently quantify impacts (e.g., effect of percentage changes in interest rates or supply chain disruptions on revenue or costs).
    - Use bullet points for large datasets or multiple data points to maintain conciseness.
    - Break paragraphs with numerous data references into bulleted lists for clarity.
    - Clearly and consistently label timeframes for each dataset (e.g., quarterly vs. yearly).
    - Consider summarizing each outlook factor (expected disruptions, macro trends, corporate strategic moves) in bullet points or a short table, making it more digestible.
    - Clearly label any projections or forecasts with their sources or assumptions to boost credibility and clarity.
    - Avoid unnecessary disclaimers.
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.
    - Do not explicitly reference the critic's feedback.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A polished, final version of the "Forward-Looking Outlook" section, ready for inclusion in the overall
    stock analysis report. The report should be free of errors, well-formatted, and present a clear,
    insightful perspective on the future of {company_name}. The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
  agent: editor

		)

		)

import os
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from stock_analyser.tools.gmail_tool import GmailTool
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

gemini_thinking = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-thinking-exp-01-21",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

gpt4_mini = LLM(
	model="gpt-4o-mini",
	api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

@CrewBase
class GmailCrew():
	"""GmailCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	@agent
	def subject_line_writer_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['subject_line_writer_agent'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			verbose=True
)

	@agent
	def gmail_draft_agent(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['gmail_draft_agent'],
			tools=[GmailTool()],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			verbose=True
)

	@task
	def subject_line_writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['subject_line_writing_task']
		)

	@task
	def gmail_draft_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['gmail_draft_task']
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the GmailCrew crew"""
		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True,
			cache=True
			# process=Process.hierarchical, # In case you wanna use that instead https://docs.crewai.com/how-to/Hierarchical/
		)

planning_task:
  description: >
    You are part of a team that is writing an analysis report about the stock of the following company: 
    
    Company name: {company_name}.
    
    Company ticker: {company_ticker}.

    Website: {company_website}

    Industry: {industry}

    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
    
    Your task is to develop a structured set of research questions to supplement the report and support an investment recommendation.

    First read the full report:

    - {market_and_industry_section}
    - {competitor_landscape_section}  
    - {financial_data_section}
    - {analyst_insights_section}
    - {warren_buffet_section}
    - {cathie_wood_section}
    - {benjamin_graham_section}
    - {risk_analysis_section}
    - {future_outlook_section}

    Then write a comprehensive research plan with maximum 5 prioritized research questions addressing information not already present in the report, for example:
    - valuation_factors: valuation metrics (P/E, P/S, P/B, etc.) compared to industry peers
    - fundamental_assessment: company's fundamental strengths and weaknesses (competitive advantages, management quality, etc.)
    - market_outlook: market trends and outlook for the company's industry
    - potential_catalysts: potential catalysts that could impact the stock price (product launches, regulatory changes, etc.)
    - technical_considerations: stock's technical indicators, support/resistance levels, and potential entry/exit points
    - other_recommendations: any other relevant recommendations, such as a staged buying approach

    The research questions should be specific, actionable, and relevant to the report's contents.
    Don't ask about information that is already present in the report.
    
  expected_output: >
    A structured research plan with up to 5 prioritized research questions to supplement the report and inform the investment recommendation.
    Output in a `ResearchQuestions` Pydantic model.
  agent: planner

research_task:
  description: >
    You are researching content for the "Investment Recommendation" section of an analysis report about the stock of the following company: 

    Company name: {company_name}.

    Company ticker: {company_ticker}.

    Website: {company_website}

    Industry: {industry}

    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.

    Use the tools you have available to you to gather information in answer to the research questions provided to you.

    Provide the answers to the research questions in the `ResearchAnswers` Pydantic model.
    

  expected_output: >
    A structured set of self-contained answers to each of the research questions, crafted for contextual independence.
    Output in the `ResearchAnswers` Pydantic model.
  agent: researcher

writing_task:
  description: >
    Draft the "Investment Recommendation" section based on the report's contents and the supplementary research provided to you.
    Clearly and emphatically state a Buy, Sell, or Hold recommendation, supported by an evidence-based rationale and justification.
    Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity. The writing should be clear, factual, compelling, and easy to understand.
    
    Here is the full report:
    - {market_and_industry_section}
    - {competitor_landscape_section}  
    - {financial_data_section}
    - {analyst_insights_section}
    - {warren_buffet_section}
    - {cathie_wood_section}
    - {benjamin_graham_section}
    - {risk_analysis_section}
    - {future_outlook_section}

  expected_output: >
    A draft of the "Investment Recommendation" section including a clear Buy/Sell/Hold recommendation supported by a concise rationale linking the key information from the report and the supplementary research.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
    Begin the section with the title: ## Investment Recommendation
  agent: writer

critic_task:
  description: >
    Critically review the draft "Investment Recommendation" section for accuracy, clarity, coherence, and completeness.
    Assess the strength of the recommendation and rationale, the relevance of catalysts, and the validity of technical considerations.
    Provide specific, actionable feedback to improve the content, presentation, and markdown formatting.
    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  expected_output: >
    A detailed critique of the draft, including:
    - Assessment of the recommendation's clarity and justification.
    - Evaluation of the rationale's strength and supporting evidence.
    - Feedback on the relevance and impact of identified catalysts.
    - Review of technical considerations (if any) for accuracy and feasibility.
    - Specific suggestions for improvement, focusing on clarity, conciseness, and data consistency.
    - The full, verbatim draft section content that you have critiqued.
  agent: critic

editing_task:
  description: >
    Refine the "Investment Recommendation" section, incorporating the critic's feedback to strengthen the justification, clarity, and conciseness of the rationale.
    Ensure all supporting information is accurate and consistent, enhancing rather than reducing the existing content.
    - The final version should be well-presented and aligned with the overall report.
    - Prioritize clarity, accuracy, and conciseness, leveraging the information provided.
    - Make full use of the provided information; do not suggest further research.
    - Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity. The writing should be clear, factual, compelling, and easy to understand.
    - Omit unnecessary disclaimers and do not explicitly reference the critic's feedback.
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.
    
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A final, polished version of the "Investment Recommendation" section, ready for inclusion in the report.
    This should be a fully revised and improved version, addressing all feedback from the critic.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
  agent: editor

import os
from crewai import Agent, Crew, Process, Task, LLM
from crewai.project import CrewBase, agent, crew, task
from stock_analyser.utils.models import ReportCritique, ResearchQuestions, ResearchAnswers
from stock_analyser.utils.constants import TIMESTAMP
from stock_analyser.tools.google_search_tool import GoogleSearchTool
from stock_analyser.tools.tavily_search import TavilySearchTool
from stock_analyser.tools.trafilatura_webscrape import TrafilaturaWebscrapeTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_news_tool import YFinanceNewsTool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_stock_kpi_tool import YFinanceStockKPITool
from stock_analyser.tools.yfinance_financials_tool import YFinanceStockFinancialsTool


from dotenv import load_dotenv
load_dotenv()

google_search_tool = GoogleSearchTool(api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_SEARCH_API_KEY"), cx=os.getenv("SEARCH_ENGINE_ID"))

gemini_pro = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-pro-exp-02-05",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_flash_lite = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-lite",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7,
	timeout=600
)

gemini_thinking = LLM(
	model="gemini/gemini-2.0-flash-thinking-exp-01-21",
	api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)

gpt4_mini = LLM(
	model="gpt-4o-mini",
	api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	temperature=0.7
)


@CrewBase
class InvestmentRecommendationCrew():
	"""InvestmentRecommendationCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'


	# ----Agents----#
	@agent
	def planner(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['planner'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def researcher(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['researcher'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			verbose=True,
			tools=[
				google_search_tool,
				YFinanceStockKPITool(),
				YFinanceStockFinancialsTool(),
				YFinanceNewsTool(),
				TrafilaturaWebscrapeTool(),
				TavilySearchTool()
			],
			max_iter=10
		)

	@agent
	def writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['writer'],
			llm=gemini_thinking,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def critic(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['critic'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def editor(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['editor'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def planning_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['planning_task'],
			output_pydantic=ResearchQuestions,
		)
	@task
	def research_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['research_task'],
			output_pydantic=ResearchAnswers,
		)

	@task
	def writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['writing_task'],
		)

	@task
	def critic_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['critic_task'],
			output_pydantic=ReportCritique
		)

	@task
	def editing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['editing_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Investment_Recommendation_section.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the InvestmentRecommendationCrew crew"""


		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents, # Automatically created by the @agent decorator
			tasks=self.tasks, # Automatically created by the @task decorator
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True
		)

research_task:
  description: >
    You are researching content for the "Market & Industry Context" section of a stock analysis report about the stock of the following company: 

    Company name: {company_name}.

    Company ticker: {company_ticker}.

    Website: {company_website}

    Industry: {industry}.

    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.

    Gather data and insights on the market and industry to populate the `MarketIndustryContextModel`.
    Focus on gathering information for the following fields:
    - `market_overview`: Overall market size and current conditions.
    - `industry_trends`: Key trends shaping the industry.
    - `industry_growth_forecasts`: Projections for industry growth, including size and demand drivers.
    - `industry_leaders`: Information about the industry leaders.
    - `competitor_landscape`: Market share and product differentiation strategies of key competitors.
    - `macroeconomic_factors`: Current economic trends (e.g., interest rates, inflation) and geopolitical factors impacting the industry.
    - `regulatory_environment`: Key regulations and policies impacting the industry.

    Use your tools and knowledge source to gather information on market size, demand drivers, market share of key competitors, and product differentiation strategies.
  expected_output: >
    A `MarketIndustryContextModel` Pydantic model with the `market_overview`, `industry_trends`, `industry_growth_forecasts`, `competitor_landscape`, `macroeconomic_factors`, and `regulatory_environment` fields populated.
  agent: researcher

writing_task:
  description: >
    Draft the "Market & Industry Context" section of the stock analysis report, incorporating the detailed findings from the research task and your knowledge source.
    Structure the section with clear headings for each subsection, presenting the data and analysis in a concise and informative manner.
    Format as markdown without code blocks, using bullet points, tables, and concise paragraphs to present the information effectively.
    Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity. The writing should be clear, factual, compelling, and easy to understand.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A complete draft of the "Market & Industry Context" section, including:
    - An introduction to the section.
    - A subsection for **Market Overview**, presenting the `market_overview`.
    - A subsection for **Industry Trends**, presenting the `industry_trends`.
    - A subsection for **Industry Growth Forecasts**, presenting the `industry_growth_forecasts`.
    - A subsection for **Industry Leaders**, presenting the `industry_leaders`.
    - A subsection for **Competitor Landscape**, presenting the `competitor_landscape`.
    - A subsection for **Macroeconomic Factors**, presenting the `macroeconomic_factors`.
    - A subsection for **Regulatory Environment**, presenting the `regulatory_environment`.
    Each subsection should clearly present the data gathered, along with insightful analysis and context.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks. Don't use emojis.
    Begin the section with the title: ## Market & Industry Context
  agent: writer

critic_task:
  description: >
    Review the drafted "Market & Industry Context" section for accuracy, clarity, coherence, and completeness.
    Provide specific, actionable feedback to improve the content and presentation. Focus on data consistency, clarity of writing and if the information is comprehensive.
    Current date (ISO format): {date_iso}.
  expected_output: >
    A detailed critique of the draft, including specific suggestions for improvement. This should include feedback on:
    - Accuracy of data and information.
    - Clarity and conciseness of writing.
    - Completeness of the content (addressing all key areas).
    - Adherence to the specified format (bullet points, table, markdown formatting).
    - Consistency of data across different parts of the section.
    You must also output the full, verbatim draft section content that you have critiqued as well.
  agent: critic

editing_task:
  description: >
    Revise the "Market & Industry Context" section, incorporating the critic's feedback to enhance clarity, accuracy, and conciseness.
    - Enhance, rather than reduce, existing content, addressing all feedback points.
    - Use only available information and your knowledge source; do not suggest further research.
    - Avoid uncertainty and ambiguity. The writing should be clear, factual, compelling, and easy to understand.
    - Employ brief transition statements or lists to summarize subtopics in longer sections.
    - Quantify impacts consistently (e.g., effects of percentage changes in interest rates or supply chain disruptions).
    - Use bullet points for conciseness when presenting large datasets or multiple data points.
    - Convert paragraphs with numerous data references into bulleted lists.
    - Label timeframes (e.g., quarterly vs. yearly) clearly and consistently.
    - Omit unnecessary disclaimers and do not explicitly reference the critic's feedback.
    - Don't include a publication date as a separate heading.
    Current date: {date_us}.
  expected_output: >
    A final, polished version of the "Market & Industry Context" section, ready for inclusion in the stock analysis report.
    This should be a fully revised and improved version, addressing all feedback from the critic.
    The output must be formatted as markdown without code blocks.
  agent: editor

@CrewBase
class MarketAndIndustryCrew():
	"""MarketAndIndustryCrew crew"""

	agents_config = 'config/agents.yaml'
	tasks_config = 'config/tasks.yaml'

	# Add knowledge_source as an __init__ parameter
	def __init__(self, knowledge_source=None):
		self.knowledge_source = knowledge_source

	# ----Agents----#
	@agent
	def researcher(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['researcher'],
			llm=gpt4_mini,
			tools=[
				YFinanceCompanyInfoTool(),
				TavilySearchTool(),
				TrafilaturaWebscrapeTool(),
				exasearch_tool,
				google_search_tool,
				YFinanceNewsTool(),
				YFinanceIndustryLeadersTool(),
			],
			verbose=True,
			max_iter=10,
			# max_rpm=10
		)

	@agent
	def writer(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['writer'],
			llm=gemini_thinking,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def critic(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['critic'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	@agent
	def editor(self) -> Agent:
		return Agent(
			config=self.agents_config['editor'],
			llm=gemini_pro,
			verbose=True
		)

	# ----Tasks----#
	@task
	def research_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['research_task'],
			output_pydantic=MarketIndustryContextModel,
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Market_and_Industry_Context_research.md"
		)

	@task
	def writing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['writing_task'],
		)

	@task
	def critic_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['critic_task'],
			output_pydantic=ReportCritique
		)

	@task
	def editing_task(self) -> Task:
		return Task(
			config=self.tasks_config['editing_task'],
			output_file=f"knowledge/{TIMESTAMP}_Market_and_Industry_Context_section.md"
		)

	@crew
	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the MarketAndIndustryCrew crew"""

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents,
			tasks=self.tasks,
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True,
			# Use the passed knowledge_source
			knowledge_sources=[self.knowledge_source] if self.knowledge_source else [],
			embedder={
				"provider": "google",
				"config": {
					"model": "models/text-embedding-004",
					"api_key": os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
				}
			}
		)

	def crew(self) -> Crew:
		"""Creates the NewsAndResearchCrew crew"""

		return Crew(
			agents=self.agents,
			tasks=self.tasks,
			process=Process.sequential,
			verbose=True
		)
